**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_feature_class", "label_zh": "LRS 網絡功能類", "label_en": "LRS Network Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input feature class that will be registered as the LRS Network. The name of the feature class must be 26 or fewer characters. The feature class must reside in a geodatabase that contains an LRS. The name of this feature class will be used as the name of the LRS Network."}, {"parameter": "lrs_name", "label_zh": "LRS 名稱", "label_en": "LRS Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The LRS name to which the new LRS Network will be registered. The LRS must reside in the same geodatabase as the LRS Network Feature Class value."}, {"parameter": "route_id_field", "label_zh": "線路ID外地", "label_en": "Route ID Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field in the LRS Network Feature Class value that will be mapped as the LRS Network route ID. This must be a string or GUID field type."}, {"parameter": "route_name_field", "label_zh": "路線名稱欄位( 可選)", "label_en": "Route Name Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "A string field in the LRS Network Feature Class value that will be mapped as the LRS Network route name. This parameter is required only when the Route ID Field Configuration parameter is set to Autogenerated Route ID."}, {"parameter": "from_date_field", "label_zh": "發自日期欄位", "label_en": "From Date Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "A date field in the LRS Network Feature Class value that will be mapped as the LRS Network from date."}, {"parameter": "to_date_field", "label_zh": "至日期欄位", "label_en": "To Date Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "A date field in the LRS Network Feature Class value that will be mapped as the LRS Network to date."}, {"parameter": "derive_from_line_network", "label_zh": "從線網( 可選)", "label_en": "Derive From Line Network (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the network will be configured as an LRS derived network. Checked—The output of this tool will be an LRS derived network. The Line Network Name parameter value must also be provided. Unchecked—The output of this tool will not be an LRS derived network. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "line_network_name", "label_zh": "網絡名稱( 可選)", "label_en": "Line Network Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the LRS line network to which the output LRS derived network will be registered. The input LRS line network must reside in the same geodatabase workspace as the LRS Network Feature Class value. This parameter is only used if the Derive From Line Network parameter is checked."}, {"parameter": "include_fields_to_support_lines", "label_zh": "包含支持行的欄位( 可選)", "label_en": "Include Fields to Support Lines (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the network will be configured to support lines. Checked—The output of this tool will be an LRS line network. The Line ID Field, Line Name Field, and Line Order Field parameter values must also be provided. Unchecked—The output of this tool will not be an LRS line network. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "line_id_field", "label_zh": "行 ID 欄位( 可選)", "label_en": "Line ID Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field in the LRS Network Feature Class value that will be mapped as the LRS Network line ID. This parameter is only used if the Include Fields to Support Lines parameter is checked. This must be a string or GUID field type and must match the field type and length of the route ID field in the centerline sequence table. It must also be the same field type as the Route ID Field parameter value."}, {"parameter": "line_name_field", "label_zh": "行名欄位( 可選)", "label_en": "Line Name Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "A string field in the LRS Network Feature Class value that will be mapped as the LRS Network line name. This parameter is only used if the Include Fields to Support Lines parameter is checked."}, {"parameter": "line_order_field", "label_zh": "線條順序欄位( 可選)", "label_en": "Line Order Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "An integer field in the LRS Network Feature Class value that will be mapped as the LRS Network line order. This parameter is only used if the Include Fields to Support Lines parameter is checked. This must be a long integer field type."}, {"parameter": "route_id_configuration", "label_zh": "路由 ID 外地配置(可選)", "label_en": "Route ID Field Configuration (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the route ID field configuration that will be used for the LRS Network. Autogenerated Route ID—The route ID field will be an automatically generated GUID. The route name can be configured as an LRS field. This is the default. Single-Field Route ID—The route ID field will be a single user-generated field. Only nonline networks are supported. Multi-Field Route ID—The route ID field will be a user-generated field concatenated from more than one field to form the route ID. Only nonline networks are supported."}, {"parameter": "individual_route_id_fields", "label_zh": "欄位(可選)", "label_en": "Field(s) (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The individual fields in the LRS Network Feature Class value that will be used to form the route ID. This parameter is only used if the Route ID Field Configuration parameter value is Multi-Field Route ID. The fields must be either string or integer field types."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.lr.CreateLRSNetworkFromExistingDataset(in_feature_class, lrs_name, route_id_field, {route_name_field}, from_date_field, to_date_field, {derive_from_line_network}, {line_network_name}, {include_fields_to_support_lines}, {line_id_field}, {line_name_field}, {line_order_field}, {route_id_configuration}, {individual_route_id_fields})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_feature_class、lrs_name、route_id_field、route_name_field、from_date_field、to_date_field、derive_from_line_network、line_network_name、include_fields_to_support_lines、line_id_field、line_name_field、line_order_field、route_id_configuration、individual_route_id_fields。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 從現有資料集創建 LRS 網絡( 遠程引用工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 配置工具et
- LRS 網絡工具

**english**　Create LRS Network From Existing Dataset

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-network-from-existing-dataset.html

**id**　71003c20990e947a7b-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-network-from-existing-dataset.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Create LRS Network From Existing Dataset，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。LRS 網絡功能類 [in_feature_class]、LRS 名稱 [lrs_name]、線路ID外地 [route_id_field]、路線名稱欄位( 可選) [route_name_field]、發自日期欄位 [from_date_field]、至日期欄位 [to_date_field]、從線網( 可選) [derive_from_line_network]、網絡名稱( 可選) [line_network_name]、包含支持行的欄位( 可選) [include_fields_to_support_lines]、行 ID 欄位( 可選) [line_id_field]、行名欄位( 可選) [line_name_field]、線條順序欄位( 可選) [line_order_field]、路由 ID 外地配置(可選) [route_id_configuration]、欄位(可選) [individual_route_id_fields]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/create-lrs-network-from-existing-dataset.html#parameters

## 110428：變量 <value> 的資料類型無效。 · 描述

**id**　7106264846b434fefa-1

**english**　110428: The variable <value> has an invalid data type.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 110401-110500

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110426-110450-110428.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110426-110450-110428.html

**text**　解釋訓練網格參數中提供的至少一個網格對于分類列的指定值具有無效類型。 標記為分類的網格必須為整型并且包含屬性表。 未標記為分類的網格必須為浮點型或者不包含屬性表。 具有錯誤類型的網格將顯示在消息中。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110426-110450-110428.html#description

使用正斜線 (/) 代替反斜線杠。

**text**　對于簡單計算，通常只需要使用表達式參數。 對于復雜的表達式，例如多行計算或邏輯運算 (if-else)，還需使用代碼塊參數。 代碼塊參數必須與表達式參數結合使用。
可從表達式參數中引用代碼塊參數中定義的變量。
例如，在代碼塊中定義大小，以計算文件夾路徑中的文件數量。 它將在執行期間從表達式參數中引用。 計算值工具輸出值為實際文件數加 5。

在需要定期備份資料的情況下，在文件夾名稱中添加時間有助于區分資料。 以下示例演示了如何向文件夾名稱添加時間戳。 time.ctime 函數以 Tue Jun 22 16:24:08 2021 等格式返回當前日期和時間。 由于不允許使用空格和標點符號，因此此返回值不能直接用作創建文件夾工具的文件夾名稱。 要將其移除，Python replace 方法的使用方式為，為每個需要替換的元素堆疊此方法。 在本示例中，得到的文件夾名稱為 TueJun221622522021。

**text**　Expression:
getAspectDir("%Input Degree%")
Code Block:
def getAspectDir(inValue):
 inValue = int(inValue)
 if inValue >= 45 and inValue < 135:
     return "East"
 elif inValue >= 135 and inValue < 225:
     return "South"
 elif inValue >= 225 and inValue < 315:
     return "West"
 else:
     return "North"

getPath(r"%Folder Path%", "%Folder Name%")
Code Block:
import os
def getPath(folderPath, folderName):
 outPath = os.path.join(folderPath, folderName)
 if not os.path.exists(outPath):
     os.makedirs(outPath)
 return outPath

**text**　要將計算值工具與接受線性距離的工具（例如緩衝區工具）結合使用，請執行以下操作：
將在代碼塊參數中返回一個距離值和一個線性單位。

## 使用 Python 進行計算值的示例 · 游標

**id**　71089afa9dc36e8491-53

**english**　Examples of using Calculate Value with Python

**chapter**　
- 幫助
- 分析和地理處理
- 地理處理
- ModelBuilder
- ModelBuilder 工具箱
- 實用工具
- 示例

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/modelbuilder-toolbox/examples-of-using-calculate-value-tool.html#408

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/modelbuilder-toolbox/examples-of-using-calculate-value-tool.html

**text**　您可以使用游標來處理資料。 游標是一個資料訪問對象，用于在表中的各個行上進行迭代或在表中插入新行。 游標有三種形式：搜索、插入和更新，通常用于讀寫資料。
要將樹種代碼轉換為其常用名稱，可以使用 UpdateCursor 來遍歷表中的每棵樹。 根據樹代碼值，為其分配常用名稱。 例如，如果一棵樹的代碼為 PIPO，則將其常用名稱黃松分配給 CommonName 欄位。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/modelbuilder-toolbox/examples-of-using-calculate-value-tool.html#408

## 使用 Python 進行計算值的示例 · 游標

**id**　71089afa9dc36e8491-54

**english**　Examples of using Calculate Value with Python

**chapter**　
- 幫助
- 分析和地理處理
- 地理處理
- ModelBuilder
- ModelBuilder 工具箱
- 實用工具
- 示例

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/modelbuilder-toolbox/examples-of-using-calculate-value-tool.html#408

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/modelbuilder-toolbox/examples-of-using-calculate-value-tool.html

**text**　Expression:
fn("%trees%")
Code Block:
def fn(trees):
 with arcpy.da.UpdateCursor(trees, ["Code", "CommonName"]) as cursor:
     for row in cursor:
         if row[0] == "PIPO":
             row[1] = "ponderosa pine"
         elif row[0] == "BEPA":
             row[1] = "paper birch"
         elif row[0] == "FAGR":
             row[1] = "American beech"
         cursor.updateRow(row)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/modelbuilder-toolbox/examples-of-using-calculate-value-tool.html#408

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/modelbuilder-toolbox/examples-of-using-calculate-value-tool.html#A03

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/modelbuilder-toolbox/examples-of-using-calculate-value-tool.html

**text**　ArcPy 還提供了對地理處理工具的訪問。 可以在代碼塊參數中調用地理處理工具。
在以下示例中，計算值工具將使用按屬性選擇圖層、復制圖徵和緩衝區工具。 這些工具將選擇 LABEL 欄位包含 HIGHWAY 一詞的所有道路，制作其副本，然后對道路進行緩衝處理。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/modelbuilder-toolbox/examples-of-using-calculate-value-tool.html#A03

## 使用 Python 進行計算值的示例 · 地理處理工具

**id**　71089afa9dc36e8491-56

**english**　Examples of using Calculate Value with Python

**chapter**　
- 幫助
- 分析和地理處理
- 地理處理
- ModelBuilder
- ModelBuilder 工具箱
- 實用工具
- 示例

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/modelbuilder-toolbox/examples-of-using-calculate-value-tool.html#A03

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/modelbuilder-toolbox/examples-of-using-calculate-value-tool.html

**text**　Expression:
fn("%Input Feature Class%")
Code Block:
def fn(InputFC):
 # To allow overwriting outputs change overwriteOutput option to True.w
 arcpy.env.overwriteOutput = True
 # Process: Select Layer By Attribute (Select Layer By Attribute) (management)
 InputFC_Layer, Count = arcpy.management.SelectLayerByAttribute(InputFC, "NEW_SELECTION", "LABEL LIKE '%HIGHWAY%'")
 # Process: Copy Features (Copy Features) (management)
 copyFeaturesOutput = "C:\\temp\\Output.gdb\\copyFeaturesOutput"
 arcpy.management.CopyFeatures(InputFC_Layer, copyFeaturesOutput)
 # Process: Buffer (Buffer) (analysis)
 bufferOutput = "C:\\temp\\Output.gdb\\bufferOutput"
 arcpy.analysis.Buffer(copyFeaturesOutput, bufferOutput, "1500 Feet")
 return bufferOutput

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/modelbuilder-toolbox/examples-of-using-calculate-value-tool.html#A03

## 翻譯事件措施(遠程參考工具) · 摘要摘要

**id**　712aca68578b95fab0-1

**english**　Translate Event Measures

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/translate-event-measures.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/translate-event-measures.html

**text**　將點或線性事件層的度量(m值)從一個線性參考方法(LRM)轉換為另一個線性事件層。該工具的一個使用實例是將事件資料從一英里基網絡轉化為一個公里基網絡。除了措施外,事件特征的路線聯系也可能在兩個LRM之間有所不同。該工具的輸出是一個特性類,具有目標的LRS網絡的正確度量和路徑關聯。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Translates the measures (m-values) of a point or line event layer from one linear referencing method (LRM) to another.\nA use case for this tool would be to translate event data from a mile-based network to a kilometer-based network.\nIn addition to measures, the route associations of the event features may also be different between the two LRMs. The output of this tool is a feature class with the correct measures and route associations of the target LRS Network.", "section": "Summary"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/translate-event-measures.html#summary

## 翻譯事件措施(遠程參考工具) · 使用率

**id**　712aca68578b95fab0-2

**english**　Translate Event Measures

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/translate-event-measures.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/translate-event-measures.html

**text**　輸入事件層必須是一個點或線性特征類。 此工具不會修改任何輸入, 并將創建特性類作為輸出。 此工具支持一次時間代表。 在運行此工具之前, 創建一個基于時間的定義查詢, 或者對輸入事件層和目標 LRS 網絡應用基于時間的選擇 。 如果為輸入層配置了時間, 也可以使用 ArcGIS Pro 中的時間滑動器 。 該工具支持將事件與存在缺口的路徑相對應。 如果目標網絡是 LRS 線條網絡, 也稱為工程網絡, 則可以翻譯輸入事件層, 即使輸入事件層的原始網絡不支持行的概念 。 輸出特性類類型- 行或點-必須與輸入事件圖層類型匹配。 輸入事件圖層的容度和分辨率設置應該與目標 LRS 網絡的容度和分辨率設置相匹配。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The input event layer must be a point or line feature class.\nThis tool does not modify either input and will create a feature class as an output.\nThis tool supports one temporal representation at a time. Create a time-based definition query or apply a time-based selection set to the input event layer as well as the target LRS Network before running this tool. The time slider in ArcGIS Pro can also be used if time is configured for both of the input layers.\nThis tool supports translating events against routes with gaps.\nIf the target network is an LRS line network, also known as an engineering network, the input event layer can be translated even if the original network of the input event layer does not support the concept of lines.\nThe output feature class type—line or point—must match the input event layer type.\nThe tolerance and resolution settings for the input event layer should match those of the target LRS Network.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/translate-event-measures.html#usage

## 翻譯事件措施(遠程參考工具) · 使用率

**id**　712aca68578b95fab0-3

**english**　Translate Event Measures

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/translate-event-measures.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/translate-event-measures.html

**text**　要識別翻譯過程中出現的匹配條件, 在輸出特征類中創建了一個名為 Loc_ Error 的欄位, 包含多匹配、 部分匹配、 無匹配和無錯誤等值。 輸入事件層的屬性欄位包含在輸出特性類中 。 如果目標 LRS 網絡包含并行路線, 請使用同步路線匹配選項 。 如果連接路徑標識的路徑與同步路線匹配, 輸入事件層必須注冊為 LRS 事件層。 此工具將在目標 LRS 網絡上對輸入事件層進行空間覆蓋, 以生成目標網絡沿線輸入事件的功能 。 這些功能將包含路徑標識并測量該位置在路徑上的位置的信息 。 輸入事件層和輸入程序目標網絡應只代表一個時間點以獲得準確輸出。如果輸入事件層代表多個時間點,則使用時間滑動器設置過濾器或使用日期列上的定義查詢。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "To identify matching conditions that occur during the translation, a field called Loc_Error is created in the output feature class containing values such as Multi-Match, Partial Match, No Match, and No Error.\nThe attribute fields of the input event layer are included in the output feature class.\nIf the target LRS Network contains concurrent routes, use the Concurrent Route Matching option.\nIf Route with matching Route ID is used with Concurrent Route Matching, the input event layer must be registered as an LRS event layer.\nThis tool will perform a spatial overlay of the input event layer onto the target LRS Network to generate features for the input event along the target network. These features will contain the Route ID and measure information of that location on the route.\nThe input event layer and the target network should only represent a single point in time to obtain an accurate output. If the input event layer represents multiple points in time, set a filter using the time slider or use a definition query on the date columns.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/translate-event-measures.html#usage
